(81)

Here we have written under any of the relevant terms the associated contribution to the modified field strength after we consider the
\mathrm{SO}(3)
truncation. The terms where only one of the generators
t_{I}
appear can always be neglected since they vanish once the trace in (
59
) is performed.

Let us now consider the second term in (
77
):

\begin{split}&{i\lambda\iota_{Y}\iota_{Y}\big(\hat{C}_{(7)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\big)}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}\\
=&-i\lambda[Y^{i},Y^{j}]\Big(\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}\hat{B}_{ij}+\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ibc}\hat{B}_{aj}+\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ijc}\hat{B}_{ab}\Big)\\
=&\,\lambda\,\mathchoice{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}Y^{Ji}Y^{Kj}\Big(\lambda Y^{I^{\prime}k}\Big(\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}\mathchoice{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.96301pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.96301pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.69165pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.77974pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{ijk}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.96301pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.96301pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.69165pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.77974pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{ijk}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.96301pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.96301pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.69165pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.77974pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{ijk}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.96301pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.96301pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.69165pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.77974pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{ijk}}}}+\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ijc}\mathchoice{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.27448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.27448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.3982pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.28441pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{abk}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.27448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.27448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.3982pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.28441pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{abk}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.27448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.27448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.3982pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.28441pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{abk}}}}{H^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.27448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.27448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.3982pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.28441pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{abk}}}}\Big)t_{I^{\prime}}\\
&+\Big(C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ibc}-\lambda Y^{I^{\prime}k}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.24945pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.24945pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.39331pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13808pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{ik}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.24945pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.24945pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.39331pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13808pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{ik}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.24945pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.24945pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.39331pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13808pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{ik}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.24945pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.24945pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.39331pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13808pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{ik}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}dbc}t_{I^{\prime}}-\lambda Y^{I^{\prime}k}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.8081pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43436pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{bk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.8081pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43436pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{bk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.8081pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43436pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{bk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.8081pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43436pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{bk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ilc}t_{I^{\prime}}\Big)\hat{B}_{aj}\Big)t_{I}\;,\end{split}

(82)

where in the last passage we have substituted the expansion for the hatted field
\hat{C}
taken from (
80
) and also expanded the 2-form
\hat{B}_{(2)}
. Since we want to retain only contributions up to order
\lambda^{2}
, we can neglect any further expansion of the hatted fields, while the only relevant effect of the pullback is in the term

\mathrm{P}\big[C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ibc}B_{aj}\big]=C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ibc}B_{aj}+2\lambda D_{b}Y^{k}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ikc}B_{aj}+\lambda D_{a}Y^{k}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ibc}B_{kj}\;.

(83)

We can single out the following contribution to
{(J_{(3)}^{(\textrm{D}6)})}_{abk}

\lambda^{2}\mathchoice{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}Y^{Ji}Y^{Kj}Y^{I^{\prime}k}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.8081pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43436pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{bk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.8081pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43436pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{bk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.8081pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43436pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{bk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.93683pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.8081pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43436pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{bk}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ilc}\hat{B}_{aj}t_{I}t_{I^{\prime}}\;,

(84)

that matches the covariant derivative of the term proportional to
g_{1}Y^{3}\chi_{2}
expected in the modified
\bar{F}_{(2)}
.

From the third term in (
77
) we get :

{\mathrm{P}\big[i\lambda^{2}\iota_{Y}\iota_{Y}\hat{C}_{(7)}\wedge\mathcal{F}\big]}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}=\,\lambda^{2}\,\mathchoice{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}Y^{Ji}Y^{Kj}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}ijc}\mathchoice{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}t_{I}t_{I^{\prime}}+\mathcal{O}(\lambda^{3})\;,

(85)

which cannot be interpreted as bringing a modification to the field strength, while the last one gives no contribution at all since the interior product should be applied to a 11-form.

Modified
F_{(4)}

In order to find the modified
\bar{F}_{(4)}
, we can start again from the expression of the field strength in the democratic formulation of type IIA (
161
). However, some modifications are needed. Our setup, indeed, differs from the one of
[
31
]
in that we are considering the presence of topological fluxes. As explained in detail in
[
32
]
, this implies a modification of the Chern-Simons terms of IIA supergravity, basically because fluxes prevent boundary terms from vanishing when an integration by parts is performed. The general solution for the field strengths
{F}_{(p)}
in presence of fluxes is then
[
22
]

\boldsymbol{F}=\differential\boldsymbol{C}+H_{(3)}\wedge\boldsymbol{C}+\boldsymbol{F}^{\textup{flux}}\wedge e^{-B_{(2)}}\;,

(86)

where bold symbols
\boldsymbol{F}
,
\boldsymbol{C}
,
\boldsymbol{F}^{\textup{flux}}
denote respectively formal sums of all the field strengths, the RR fields and the constant flux contributions to the field strengths (for any field strength
{F}_{(p)}
on the LHS we have to consider only the terms with the appropriate degree in the above expansion on the RHS).

In this case, this means that
F_{(4)}
(before the contributions coming from the open string sector) can be written as

F_{(4)}=\differential C_{(3)}-F_{(2)}^{\textup{flux}}\wedge B_{(2)}+\frac{1}{2}F_{(0)}^{\textup{flux}}B_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}+F_{(4)}^{\textup{flux}}\;.

(87)

In our setup, namely the orientifold projection and the
\mathrm{SO}(3)
truncation, the only non-vanishing components of the field strength are

F_{(4)}=\frac{1}{4!}F_{aibj}\,e^{a}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{j}=\frac{1}{4!}\,f_{4}\,\varepsilon_{abc}\,\varepsilon_{ijk}\,\delta^{ck}\,e^{a}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{j}\;.

(88)

If we consider the following expressions

\big(\differential C_{(3)}\big)_{aibj}=-\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{ab}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{ab}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{ab}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{ab}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}C_{cij}-\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}C_{cab}+2\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.05121pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.05121pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.88843pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.49171pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{aj}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.05121pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.05121pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.88843pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.49171pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{aj}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.05121pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.05121pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.88843pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.49171pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{aj}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.05121pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.05121pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.88843pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.49171pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{aj}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}C_{kib}\;,

(89)

\big(F_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}-\frac{1}{2}F_{(0)}B_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}\big)_{aibj}=2F_{ai}B_{bj}-F_{(0)}B_{ai}B_{bj}\;,

(90)

and compare them with (
88
), taking into account also the fact that we have already obtained the full expression for the modified field strengths
\bar{F}_{(2)}
and
\bar{F}_{(0)}
, we obtain

f_{4}=\,a_{1}+\bar{c}_{1}\chi_{1}-b_{1}\chi-2c_{1}\chi_{1}-2(a_{2}+c_{1}Y^{2}+g_{1}\mathcal{A}Y^{2}-a_{3}\chi_{2}+g_{1}Y^{3}\chi_{2})\chi_{2}-(a_{3}-g_{1}Y^{3})\chi_{2}^{2}\;.

(91)

On the other hand, the modified field strength
\tilde{F}_{(4)}
that we expect from the scalar potential in the 4-dimensional gauged supergravity theory suggests that the scalar
f_{4}
is replaced by

\begin{split}\tilde{f}_{4}=&\,a_{1}-b_{1}\chi-2c_{1}\chi_{1}+\bar{c}_{1}\chi_{1}-2a_{2}\chi_{2}-2c_{1}Y^{2}\chi_{2}-2g_{1}\mathcal{A}Y^{2}\chi_{2}\\
&+a_{3}\chi_{2}^{2}-g_{1}Y^{3}\chi_{2}^{2}-g_{0}Y-g_{1}\mathcal{A}^{2}Y-\frac{c_{0}Y^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{A}Y(2c_{1}+\bar{c}_{1})\;.\end{split}

(92)

The missing terms in
f_{4}
, namely

\Delta f_{4}=\tilde{f}_{4}-f_{4}=-g_{0}Y-g_{1}\mathcal{A}^{2}Y-\frac{c_{0}Y^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{A}Y(2c_{1}+\bar{c}_{1})\;,

(93)

should then come out from the WZ action. In order to find them, or equivalently to find the modified Bianchi identity for the field strength
F_{(4)}
, we have to look at the couplings in the Wess-Zumino action that involve the
{C}_{(5)}
field:

\begin{split}\mu_{\textrm{D}6}\int_{\textup{WV}(\textrm{D}6)}\Tr\{\mathrm{P}&\bigg[\hat{C}_{(5)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}+\frac{i\lambda}{2}\iota_{Y}\iota_{Y}\Big(\hat{C}_{(5)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\Big)+\lambda\,\hat{C}_{(5)}\wedge\mathcal{F}\\
&+i\lambda^{2}\iota_{Y}\iota_{Y}\Big(\hat{C}_{(5)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\Big)\wedge\mathcal{F}+\mathcal{O}(\lambda^{3})\bigg]\bigg\}\quad,\end{split}

(94)

aiming at isolating a current
J_{(5)}^{(\textrm{D}6)}
appearing in

(95)

(96)

Hence, we look for components of the current of type
{(J_{(5)}^{(\textrm{D}6)})}_{abcij}
, which should be coupled with a
{(C_{(5)})}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}k}
field.

The first term in (
94
) can be rewritten as

\begin{split}\mathrm{P}&\big[\hat{C}_{(5)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\big]_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}=\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}a}\hat{B}_{bc}+\lambda D_{a}Y^{i}\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}i}\hat{B}_{bc}+\lambda D_{b}Y^{i}\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}a}\hat{B}_{ic}\\

&+\lambda^{2}Y^{I^{\prime}k}\Big(-\underbrace{\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.75781pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.75781pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.29529pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78233pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{ak}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.75781pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.75781pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.29529pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78233pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{ak}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.75781pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.75781pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.29529pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78233pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{ak}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.75781pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.75781pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.29529pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78233pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{l}}}_{{{ak}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}l}B_{bi}B_{jc}}_{g_{1}Y^{3}\chi_{2}^{2}}+C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}i}B_{ja}H_{bck}\Big)t_{I^{\prime}}\Big)t_{I}\;.\end{split}

(98)

From the third term in (
94
) we can obtain

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{P}\big[\lambda\,\hat{C}_{(5)}\wedge\mathcal{F}\big]}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}\\
=&\;\lambda\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}a}\mathcal{F}_{bc}+\lambda^{2}D_{a}Y^{i}\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}i}\mathcal{F}_{bc}\\

=&\;\lambda (100)

Using this result, we can isolate in the previous formula the contributions containing at least a metric flux, and then contributing to our modified Bianchi identity:

\begin{split}&-2\lambda^{2}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2782pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05585pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{ai}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2782pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05585pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{ai}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2782pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05585pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{ai}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2782pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05585pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{ai}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}j}\Big(\underbrace{Y^{Ii}{\mathchoice{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\ &-2\lambda^{2}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}j}\underbrace{\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2782pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05585pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{ai}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2782pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05585pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{ai}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2782pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05585pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{ai}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.16693pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2782pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.05585pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{ai}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}Y^{Ii}\mathcal{A}^{I^{\prime}}_{d}}_{\bar{c}_{1}Y\mathcal{A}\;,\;c_{1}Y\mathcal{A}}t_{I}t_{I^{\prime}}+\lambda^{2}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}i}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\underbrace{\mathchoice{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{A}^{J}_{a}Y^{Ki}\mathcal{A}^{I^{\prime}}_{d}}_{g_{1}Y\mathcal{A}^{2}}t_{I}t_{I^{\prime}}\;.\end{split}

(101)

The last term in (
94
) is already of order
\lambda^{2}
, then we can neglect both the expansion of the fields and the pullback:

\begin{split}&{\mathrm{P}\big[i\lambda^{2}\iota_{Y}\iota_{Y}\Big(\hat{C}_{(5)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\Big)\wedge\mathcal{F}\big]}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}\\
=&\,\lambda^{2}\,\mathchoice{g^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{JK}}}}{g^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{JK}}}}{g^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{JK}}}}{g^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{JK}}}}Y^{Ji}Y^{Kj}C_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}i}B_{ja}\mathcal{F}_{bc}t_{I}+\mathcal{O}(\lambda^{3})\;,\end{split}

(102)

then it is expected that it gives no contribution to the modified Bianchi identity.

Modified
F_{(6)}

According to the formula (
86
) for the R-R field strengths of type IIA supergravity, the full expression for
F_{(6)}
is

F_{(6)}=\differential C_{(5)}+H_{(3)}\wedge C_{(3)}-F_{(4)}^{\textup{flux}}\wedge B_{(2)}+\frac{1}{2}F_{(2)}^{\textup{flux}}\wedge B_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}-\frac{1}{3!}F_{(0)}^{\textup{flux}}B_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}+F_{(6)}^{\textup{flux}}\;.

(103)

In the
\mathrm{SO}(3)
truncation, the field strength has the form

F_{(6)}=\frac{1}{6!}F_{aibjck}\,e^{a}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{c}\wedge e^{k}=\frac{1}{6!}\,f_{6}\,\varepsilon_{abc}\,\varepsilon_{ijk}\,e^{a}\wedge e^{i}\wedge e^{b}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{c}\wedge e^{k}\;.

(104)

In order to derive the expression for the scalar
f_{6}
, we can write in components

\big(H_{(3)}\wedge C_{(3)}\big)_{aibjck}=H_{ijk}C_{abc}+3H_{abk}C_{ijc}

(105)

\begin{split}&\big(F_{(4)}\wedge B_{(2)}-\frac{1}{2}F_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}+\frac{1}{3!}F_{(0)}B_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}\wedge B_{(2)}\big)_{aibjck}\\
=&\,3\,F_{aibj}B_{ck}-\frac{6}{2}F_{ai}B_{bj}B_{ck}+\frac{6}{3!}F_{(0)}B_{ai}B_{bj}B_{ck}\;,\end{split}

(106)

so that we get

f_{6}=\,a_{0}-b_{0}\chi+3c_{0}\chi_{1}-3\chi_{2}\tilde{f}_{4}-3\chi_{2}^{2}\tilde{f}_{2}-\chi_{2}^{3}\tilde{F}_{(0)}\;,

(107)

where we have to consider the previous results for the modified field strengths of rank
p<6
. After these replacements, we can compare
f_{6}
with the modified field strength computed from the gauged supergravity potential

\begin{split}\tilde{f}_{6}=&\,a_{0}-b_{0}\chi+3c_{0}\chi_{1}-3a_{1}\chi_{2}+3g_{0}Y\chi_{2}+\frac{3}{2}c_{0}Y^{2}\chi_{2}+3b_{1}\chi\chi_{2}+6c_{1}\chi_{1}\chi_{2}-3\bar{c}_{1}\chi_{1}\chi_{2}\\
&+3a_{2}\chi_{2}^{2}+3c_{1}Y^{2}\chi_{2}^{2}-a_{3}\chi_{2}^{3}+g_{1}Y^{3}\chi_{2}^{3}+3\mathcal{A}g_{0}+g_{1}\mathcal{A}^{3}+\frac{3}{2}\mathcal{A}^{2}(\bar{c}_{1}+2g_{1}Y\chi_{2})\\
&+\frac{3}{2}\mathcal{A}Y(c_{0}+\chi_{2}(2c_{1}+\bar{c}_{1}+2g_{1}Y\chi_{2}))\;.\end{split}

(108)

Hence, the terms that we should be able to read off from the non-abelian brane action correspond to

\Delta f_{6}=\tilde{f}_{6}-f_{6}=\frac{3}{2}\bar{c}_{1}\mathcal{A}^{2}+3\mathcal{A}g_{0}+\mathcal{A}^{3}g_{1}+\frac{3}{2}c_{0}\mathcal{A}Y\;.

(109)

The contributions to the WZ action that we can analyze concerning
\tilde{F}_{(6)}
are

\begin{split}\mu_{\textrm{D}6}\int_{\textup{WV}(\textrm{D}6)}\Tr\{\mathrm{P}&\bigg[\frac{1}{2}\hat{C}_{(3)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}+\frac{i\lambda}{3!}\iota_{Y}\iota_{Y}\Big(\hat{C}_{(3)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\Big)\\
&+\lambda\,\hat{C}_{(3)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\wedge\mathcal{F}+i\lambda^{2}\iota_{Y}\iota_{Y}\Big(\hat{C}_{(3)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\Big)\wedge\mathcal{F}\\
&+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\hat{C}_{(3)}\wedge\mathcal{F}\wedge\mathcal{F}+\mathcal{O}(\lambda^{3})\bigg]\bigg\}\quad.\end{split}

(110)

Let us focus on the last contribution. Writing it in components, we can select a term of the form

\Big(\hat{C}_{(3)}\wedge\mathcal{F}\wedge\mathcal{F}\Big)_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}=\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\mathcal{F}_{\mu_{3}a}\mathcal{F}_{bc}=\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\big(\partial_{\mu_{3}}\mathcal{A}_{a}\big)\mathcal{F}_{bc}+\ldots\;.

(111)

After an integration by parts, it gives back in the WZ action a contribution
\partial_{\mu_{3}}\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\mathcal{A}_{a}\mathcal{F}_{bc}
, that we can interpret as a coupling of the derivative of
{C}_{(3)}
with the modified field strength
\tilde{F}_{(6)}
. Using the expression (
100
) for the non-abelian field strength, we get

\partial_{\mu_{3}}\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\Big(\underbrace{\mathcal{A}_{a}^{I}{\mathchoice{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}}^{\textup{(flux)}}}_{g_{0}\mathcal{A}}-\underbrace{\mathcal{A}_{a}^{I}\mathchoice{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.98946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.98946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[10.62546pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.42531pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I^{\prime}}}}_{{{J^{\prime}K^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.98946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.98946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[10.62546pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.42531pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I^{\prime}}}}_{{{J^{\prime}K^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.98946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.98946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[10.62546pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.42531pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I^{\prime}}}}_{{{J^{\prime}K^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[15.98946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[15.98946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[10.62546pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[8.42531pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I^{\prime}}}}_{{{J^{\prime}K^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.9357pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.00096pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.27568pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{A}^{J^{\prime}}_{b}\mathcal{A}^{K^{\prime}}_{c}}_{g_{1}\mathcal{A}^{3}}+\underbrace{\mathcal{A}_{a}^{I}\mathchoice{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\omega^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{d}}}_{{{bc}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{A}_{d}^{I^{\prime}}}_{\bar{c}_{1}\mathcal{A}^{2}}\Big)t_{I}t_{I^{\prime}}\;.

(112)

With a similar procedure, starting from the third term in (
110
), we can derive from

\Big(\hat{C}_{(3)}\wedge\hat{B}_{(2)}\wedge\mathcal{F}\Big)_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}\mu_{3}abc}=\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\mathcal{F}_{\mu_{3}a}\hat{B}_{bc}=\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\big(\partial_{\mu_{3}}\mathcal{A}_{a}\big)\hat{B}_{bc}+\ldots

(113)

a coupling
\partial_{\mu_{3}}\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\mathcal{A}_{a}\hat{B}_{bc}
that finally gives, if we replace the expansion for the hatted field
\hat{B}_{(2)}
,

\partial_{\mu_{3}}\hat{C}_{\mu_{0}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}}\lambda\Big(\underbrace{\mathcal{A}_{a}^{I}Y^{I^{\prime}i}H_{bci}}_{c_{0}\mathcal{A}Y}\Big)t_{I}t_{I^{\prime}}\;.

(114)

4.4
Reduction of the DBI action

The DBI action (
58
) is responsible for contributions to the scalar potential in 4 dimensions. We expand the metric and the B-field around the position
y^{Ii}=\lambda Y^{Ii}
(static gauge), at first order in
\lambda
. Furthermore, we only keep the fluxes with non-contracted legs in the worldvolume directions of the D6-brane. We obtain:

\displaystyle\hat{G}_{ab}=\rho\sigma^{2}\delta_{ab}\quad,\quad\hat{G}_{ai}

\displaystyle=0\qquad\quad,\quad\hat{G}_{ij}=\rho\sigma^{-2}\delta_{ij}\quad,

(115)

\displaystyle\hat{B}_{ab}=\lambda c_{0}\ \varepsilon_{abk}Y^{k}\quad,\quad\hat{B}_{ai}

\displaystyle=\chi_{2}\delta_{ai}\quad,\quad\hat{B}_{ij}=0\qquad\qquad.

(116)

According to the definition (
179
), one can compute the fields

\hat{E}_{ab}=\rho\sigma^{2}\delta_{ab}+\lambda c_{0}\varepsilon_{abk}Y^{k}\quad,\quad\hat{E}_{ai}=-\hat{E}_{ia}=\chi_{2}\delta_{ai}\quad,\quad\hat{E}_{ij}=\rho\sigma^{-2}\delta_{ij}\quad.

(117)

Due to the definition (
178
), we obtain that

\mathchoice{\mathbb{Q}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\mathbb{Q}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\mathbb{Q}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\mathbb{Q}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}=\mathchoice{\delta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\delta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][ with

\mathchoice{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}=\rho\sigma^{-2}g_{1}{\varepsilon_{IJ}}^{L}Y^{Ii}\mathchoice{Y^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{Y^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt The antisymmetry property of
{\varepsilon_{IJ}}^{L}
leads to
\mathchoice{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}=0
, hence

\sqrt{\det\mathchoice{\mathbb{Q}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\mathbb{Q}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\mathbb{Q}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\mathbb{Q}^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}}=\ 1-\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\Bigl(\frac{{A^{i}}_{j}{A^{j}}_{i}}{2}\Bigr)+\dots=1+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\rho^{2}\sigma^{-4}{g_{1}}^{2}{Y}^{2}\sum_{I=1}^{\mathfrak{N}}{t_{I}}^{2}+\dots\quad.

(118)

In the last step, the definitions (
40
) and (
41
) have been used.

In order to find the contribution of (
177
) to the DBI action, let us employ (
182
) to compute

P[\hat{E}_{ab}]=\hat{E}_{ab}+\lambda D_{a}Y^{k}\hat{E}_{kb}+\lambda D_{b}Y^{k}\hat{E}_{ak}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}D_{a}Y^{i}D_{b}Y^{j}\hat{E}_{ij}\quad.

(119)

In particular, by using the expressions (
117
) and the definition (
183
), one obtains

\displaystyle D_{a}Y^{k}\hat{E}_{kb}

\displaystyle=-\chi_{2}\delta_{kb}\bigl(c_{1}{\varepsilon_{am}}^{k}Y^{m}-g_{1}{\varepsilon_{IJ}}^{K}{\mathcal{A}_{a}}^{I}Y^{Jk}t_{K}\bigr)\quad,

\displaystyle D_{b}Y^{k}\hat{E}_{ak}

\displaystyle=\chi_{2}\delta_{ak}\bigl(c_{1}{\varepsilon_{bm}}^{k}Y^{m}-g_{1}{\varepsilon_{IJ}}^{K}{\mathcal{A}_{b}}^{I}Y^{Jk}t_{K}\bigr)\quad,

\displaystyle D_{a}Y^{i}D_{b}Y^{j}\hat{E}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\rho\sigma^{-2}\bigl(c_{1}{\varepsilon_{am}}^{i}Y^{m}-g_{1}{\varepsilon_{IJ}}^{K}{\mathcal{A}_{a}}^{I}Y^{Ji}t_{K}\bigr)\bigl(c_{1}{\varepsilon_{bl}}^{j}Y^{l}-g_{1}{\varepsilon_{MN}}^{L}{\mathcal{A}_{b}}^{M}Y^{Nj}t_{L}\bigr)\delta_{ij}\quad.

Accordingly, (
119
) can be recast in

P[\hat{E}_{ab}]=\rho\sigma^{2}\delta_{ab}+\lambda\hat{E}^{(1)}_{ab}+\lambda^{2}\hat{E}^{(2)}_{ab}\quad,

(120)

with

\displaystyle\hat{E}^{(1)}_{ab}=c_{0}\varepsilon_{abk}Y^{k}+

\displaystyle 2\chi_{2}\bigl(c_{1}\varepsilon_{abm}Y^{m}-g_{1}{\varepsilon_{IJ}}^{K}{\mathcal{A}_{[b|}}^{I}{Y^{J}}_{|a]}t_{K}\bigr)\quad,

\displaystyle\hat{E}^{(2)}_{ab}=\frac{1}{2}D_{a}Y^{i}D_{b}Y^{j}\hat{E}_{ij}\quad.

Moreover,

{({\mathbb{Q}}^{-1})^{i}}_{j}=\mathchoice{\delta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\delta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\delta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\delta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}-\lambda\mathchoice{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[ 1319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}})+\dots\quad.

This enters the matrix
\mathbb{M}_{MN}
defined in (
177
), along with

\displaystyle P[\hat{E}_{ai}]

\displaystyle=\hat{E}_{ai}+\lambda D_{a}Y^{k}\hat{E}_{ki}=\chi_{2}\delta_{ai}+\lambda\hat{E}^{(1)}_{ai}\quad,

\displaystyle P[\hat{E}_{jb}]

\displaystyle=\hat{E}_{jb}+\lambda D_{b}Y^{k}\hat{E}_{jk}=-\chi_{2}\delta_{jb}+\lambda\hat{E}^{(1)}_{bj}\quad.

where we make use of

\hat{E}^{(1)}_{ai}=\rho\sigma^{-2}\bigl(c_{1}\varepsilon_{iam}Y^{m}-g_{1}{\varepsilon_{IJ}}^{L}{\mathcal{A}_{a}}^{I}Y^{Ji}t_{L}\bigr)\quad.

(121)

We can then calculate

P[\hat{E}_{ai}({\mathbb{Q}}^{-1}-\delta)^{ij}\hat{E}_{jb}]=\rho\sigma^{-2}\bigl[\lambda{\chi_{2}}^{2}\delta_{ai}A^{ij}\delta_{jb}+\lambda^{2}\bigl(2\chi_{2}\hat{E}^{(1)}_{[a|i}A^{ij}\delta_{j|b]}-{\chi_{2}}^{2}{A^{i}}_{l}A^{lj}\delta_{ai}\delta_{jb}\bigr)\bigr]+\dots

By also writing
\mathcal{F}_{ab}
as in (
100
)

\mathcal{F}_{ab}=\bigl(g_{0}+{\bar{c}_{1}}\mathcal{A}+g_{1}\mathcal{A}^{2}\bigr){\varepsilon_{ab}}^{I}t_{I}\quad,

(122)

one can compute the matrix
\mathbb{M}_{MN}
. The relevant terms for the potential, up to second order in
\lambda
, will be

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\tau^{-2}g_{\mu\nu}+\dots\quad,

\displaystyle\mathbb{M}_{ab}

\displaystyle=\rho\sigma^{2}\delta_{ab}+\lambda{\mathbb{M}^{(1)}}_{ab}+\lambda^{2}{\mathbb{M}^{(2)}}_{ab}+\dots\quad,

with

\displaystyle{\mathbb{M}^{(1)}}_{ab}

\displaystyle=\hat{E}^{(1)}_{ab}+\rho\sigma^{-2}{\chi_{2}}^{2}\delta_{ai}A^{ij}\delta_{jb}+\mathcal{F}_{ab}\quad,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{M}^{(2)}}_{ab}

\displaystyle=\hat{E}^{(2)}_{ab}+2\chi_{2}\hat{E}^{(1)}_{[a|i}A^{ij}\delta_{j|b]}-{\chi_{2}}^{2}{A^{i}}_{l}A^{lj}\delta_{ai}\delta_{jb}\quad.

Furthermore, since
{\mathbb{M}^{(1)}}_{ab}
is antisymmetric in
(a,b)
,

\sqrt{\det\mathbb{M}_{MN}}=\sqrt{-g^{(4)}}\tau^{-4}\rho^{3/2}\sigma^{3}\Bigl(1+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{2}\rho^{-1}\sigma^{-2}{{\mathbb{M}^{(2)}}^{a}}_{a}-\frac{\lambda^{2}}{4}\rho^{-2}\sigma^{-4}{{\mathbb{M}^{(1)}}^{a}}_{b}{{\mathbb{M}^{(1)}}^{b}}_{a}\Bigr)+\dots\quad.

(123)

The calculation of the DBI action for each D6-brane finally requires to insert (
48
), (
118
) and (
123
) in (
58
) and to exploit the condition (
181
) on the gauge generators

[t_{I},t_{J}]=-i{g_{IJ}}^{K}t_{K}\quad,\quad\mathrm{Tr}[t_{I},t_{J}]=\delta_{IJ}\quad.

(124)

The setup under study also contains an orientifold plane
\mathrm{O}6^{-}
, whose DBI and WZ actions are given by (
60
) and (
61
). The contribution of O6-planes in the DBI reduction only appears at the zeroth order in
\lambda
and can be obtained by using (
46
) and (
48
) in (
60
). Both considering the latter and the contribution of the D6-branes (
58
), the DBI potential reads

V_{\mathrm{DBI}}=V^{(0)}_{\mathrm{DBI}}+\lambda^{2}V^{(2)}_{\mathrm{DBI}}+\dots\quad.

(125)

In particular, both D6-branes and O6-orientifold planes give rise to

V_{\mathrm{DBI}}^{(0)}=\sigma^{3}\tau^{-3}\bigl(N_{D6}T_{D6}+T_{\mathrm{O}6}\bigr)\quad.

(126)

This can be rewritten on grounds of the tadpole cancellation condition. Indeed, the modified Bianchi identity (
166
) in presence of D6/O6 sources reads

\mathrm{d}F_{(2)}-H_{(3)}\wedge F_{(0)}=Q_{6}\mathrm{vol}_{\mathcal{M}_{3}^{(a)}}\quad,

(127)

with on the right hand side an effective current density
Q_{6}
and the volume of the submanifold of the three compact directions along the worldvolume of the D6-brane. In particular,

Q_{6}=\frac{1}{2\pi}\bigl(N_{\mathrm{D}6}\mu_{\mathrm{D}6}+N_{\mathrm{O}6}\mu_{\mathrm{O}6}\bigr)\quad.

(128)

Working, for simplicity, in the instance such that

T_{\mathrm{D}6}=\mu_{\mathrm{D}6}\,=\,2\pi\quad,\quad T_{\mathrm{O}6^{-}}=\mu_{\mathrm{O}6^{-}}\,=\,-4\pi\quad,

(129)

and making the corresponding fluxes explicit, (
127
) reads

a_{3}b_{0}-3a_{2}c_{1}=(N_{\mathrm{D}6}-2N_{\mathrm{O}6^{-}}\bigr)

(130)

hence

V^{(0)}_{\mathrm{DBI}}=\sigma^{3}\tau^{-3}\bigl(\tilde{{F}}_{(0)}\tilde{b}_{0}-3\tilde{f}_{2}c_{1}\bigr)\quad.

(131)

The zeroth order in
\lambda
of the potential arising from the gauged supergravity is fully recovered by using in (
131
) the WZ-modified fluxes (
69
), (
74
) and the modified metric flux

\tilde{b}_{0}=b_{0}-3b_{1}\chi_{2}\quad.

(132)

This arises from the definition
H_{(3)}=\differential B_{(2)}
and corresponds to all terms in the potential scaling as
\tau^{-2}\rho^{-3}\sigma^{6}
(also see (
54
) and Table
3
).
Let us notice that
b_{1}
and
c_{1}
fluxes do not get corrections by the WZ and DBI actions, as the potential has no other terms with the same scaling as those in the (
52
) and quadratic in these fluxes. This is why in (
131
) the flux
c_{1}
is not modified.

At further order in
\lambda
instead, only D6-branes can give rise to non-trivial terms in the DBI action. Due to (
123
) and (
118
), one finds that

V^{(2)}_{\mathrm{DBI}}=\frac{\sigma^{3}}{\tau^{3}}\mathrm{Tr}\Bigl[\frac{1}{2}\rho^{-1}\sigma^{-2}{{\mathbb{M}^{(2)}}^{a}}_{a}-\frac{1}{4}\rho^{-2}\sigma^{-4}{{\mathbb{M}^{(1)}}^{a}}_{b}{{\mathbb{M}^{(1)}}^{b}}_{a}-\frac{1}{4}{A^{i}}_{j}{A^{j}}_{i}\Bigr]\quad,

(133)

with the trace meant on the gauge generators as in (
181
).

If we consider all terms arising from reduction of the DBI action (
125
) up to the second order in
\lambda
and we add them to the bulk action
V_{\textup{Bulk}}
(
57
) with all the field strengths replaced with the modified ones, we get the full potential of our theory, which perfectly matches with the scalar potential (
29
) coming from the
4D
gauged supergravity description, once we consider the proper dictionary between scalar degrees of freedom in the
\mathrm{SO}(3)
truncation (
13
), (
14
).

5
Analysis of vacuum solutions

Vacuum solutions are found by imposing the extremality condition of the potential (
29
) with respect to the scalar fields of the theory (collectively named here as
\Sigma
)

\partial_{\Sigma}V(\Sigma)|_{\Sigma_{0}}=0\quad,

(134)

where
\Sigma_{0}
represents the corresponding vacuum expectation values. We work in the
flux picture

[
23
]
, namely we fix one point (or a subset) in the scalar manifold and then we look for the (real) flux values that minimize the potential. These critical points also need to satisfy the quadratic constraints (
38
) arising from the consistency of the gauged supergravity formulation. The set of polynomials associated to equations (
134
) and (
38
), always quadratic in the fluxes, generate a polynomial ideal
I
, which can be studied with the aid of algebraic geometry techniques, as in
[
33
,
34
,
23
]
. In particular, we exploit the Gianni-Trager-Zacharias (GTZ) algorithm
[
35
]
to identify the decomposition of
I
into primary ideals
J_{i}

I=J_{1}\cap\dots\cap J_{n}\quad,

whose existence is ensured by the Lasker-Noether theorem.
This task has been accomplished by making use of the
Singular
software
[
36
]
. If we denote with
\mathcal{Z}(I)
the set of common zeros of polynomials in the ideal
I
, which is the same as the set of zeros of the generating polynomials, we always have

\mathcal{Z}\Big(\bigcap_{i=1}^{n}J_{i}\Big)=\bigcup_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{Z}(J_{i})\quad.

Therefore we can find the real roots of the ideal
I
by finding independently those of its primary ideals
J_{i}
, or equivalently of the associated primes
\sqrt{J_{i}}
.

5.1
Solutions in the origin

To begin with, we look for vacuum solutions restricting to the origin of the scalar manifold,
i.e.
, setting to zero both the open and the closed string scalars.

This is just a starting point, because the approach of going to the origin is not completely general in our case: we cannot perform arbitrary transformations from the duality group that simultaneously rotate the embedding tensor and the point in the scalar manifold, since a generic rotation would not leave invariant the set of flux components that we have chosen to turn on.

Looking at the expression of the scalar potential in the origin, we can see that, among the open string fluxes, it does not depend on the structure constants of the Yang-Mills gauge group
\mathchoice{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
, then the only possibility to find new vacua that solve the quadratic constraint (
43
) is to have a non-vanishing field strength
\mathchoice{\mathcal{F}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\mathcal{F}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}
. Critical points of this kind are actually found: the only possible solutions are 8 1-dimensional extrema, which can be grouped into two families of four 1-parameter solutions each, differing only in the choice of two signs

(138)

In the case of our
\mathrm{SO}(3)
truncation, the diagonalized gravitini mass matrix takes the form

A_{1}=\text{diag}(\kappa_{1},\kappa_{2},\kappa_{2},\kappa_{2})\;,

(139)

due to the splitting of the
\mathrm{SU}(4)
with respect to the action of
\mathrm{SO}(3)
, so that the possible amounts of supersymmetry preserved are
\mathcal{N}=4
,
\mathcal{N}=3
,
\mathcal{N}=1
or
\mathcal{N}=0
depending on whether the absolute values of
\kappa_{1}
and
\kappa_{2}
coincide with
\sqrt{-3V}
.

Alternatively, the preserved supersymmetry can be investigated by finding the number of null eigenvalues of the fermionic shift matrix
A_{2}^{ij}
. In Table
6
we show the necessary conditions on the fluxes for the solutions in the origin to preserve different amounts of supersymmetry.

\mathcal{N}=1

\mathcal{N}=3

\mathcal{N}=4

\begin{aligned} a_{0}&=3a_{2}-3b_{1}+6c_{1}-3\bar{c}_{1}\\
a_{3}&=3a_{1}-b_{0}-3c_{0}\end{aligned}

\begin{aligned} a_{0}&=-a_{2}+b_{1}-2c_{1}-3\bar{c}_{1}\\
a_{3}&=-a_{1}-b_{0}+c_{0}\end{aligned}

\begin{aligned} a_{0}&=-3\bar{c}_{1},\quad&a_{1}=c_{0}\\
a_{2}&=b_{1}-2c_{1},\quad&a_{3}=b_{0}\end{aligned}

According to the spinor equations (
138
), or equivalently to the conditions in table
6
, all the solutions in families
\mathbf{A}
and
\mathbf{B}
are non-supersymmetric.
Indeed, requiring a non-trivial vacuum in the origin which partially preserves supersymmetry gives us back 2 solutions with residual
\mathcal{N}=1
having vanishing
g_{0}
and free value for
g_{1}
, which are the immediate generalization of the solutions already appearing in
[
23
]
,
[
28
]
(named as family
1
with choice of the sign
s_{2}=+1
and arbitrary
s_{1}
), that we report in Table
7
for completeness.

In order to understand the stability properties of each solution, it is not enough to determine the masses of the
\mathrm{SO}(3)
-invariant scalar fields from second-order derivatives of the scalar potential. Indeed, a critical point could be made unstable by fluctuations of any of the
\mathcal{N}=4
scalars, including those ones that are set to zero by the truncation. The computation of the full mass spectra for any of the solutions can be performed adopting the formulation of
[
37
]
. Stability of an
\text{AdS}_{4}
solutions is established if all the mass eigenvalues satisfy the B. F. bound given by

m^{2}\geq-\frac{3}{4}\;.

(140)

In the case of solutions of type
\mathbf{A}
and
\mathbf{B}
, the masses do not depend on the signs
s_{1}
,
s_{2}
and for both families the lowest eigenvalue does not satisfy the B.F. bound, then all these critical points are not stable. Further details about the complete set of normalized masses can be found in the appendix
C
.

5.2
Out of the origin

Moving out of the origin of the moduli space, in particular allowing
the open string scalars to have a non-vanishing value, we find 16 new critical points of the potential, all placed away from the origin only along the
\mathcal{A}
direction. As in the previous case, they can be arranged into families of 4 solutions depending on two signs
s_{1}
,
s_{2}
.

Solution

a_{0}
